- company code = blank

## @testsubject117

I can't answer that question, but you probably can by scanning the testsubject117/AMiDataStore repo and reviewing the gwbasic dos files

## Copilot

Yes â€” I found the legacy behavior.

## Direct answer
When a check is deleted, the old GW-BASIC code resets each linked invoice in `INVOICE.CHK` by setting only:

- `FLAG$ = "J"`

It does **not** clear amount or company code in the delete path. That behavior appears in the legacy delete routine here:

```basic name=LL.L url=https://github.com/testsubject117/AMiDataStore/blob/fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82c/LL.L#L3799-L3950
3799 REM #########================================================##########             #######  DELETE CHECK & RESET INVOICE TO UNPAID         ###########             ########===============================================############
3800 PRINT"Purging part of .INV file...":Q3$=""
3810 OPEN "CHECK.INV" FOR INPUT AS #2
3820 OPEN "CHECK.TMP" FOR OUTPUT LOCK WRITE AS #1
3830 IF EOF(2) THEN RESET:GOTO 3880
3840 INPUT#2,N2$,CNUM$,RF$,DT$,CHKAMT,CT
3845 FOR X=1 TO CT:INPUT#2,HOLD(X):NEXT
3850 IF CNUM$=C1$ AND N2$=N0$ THEN Q3$="Y":GOTO 3910
3860 WRITE#1,N2$;CNUM$;RF$;DT$;CHKAMT;CT
3865 FOR X=1 TO CT:WRITE#1,INT(HOLD(X)):NEXT
3870 GOTO 3830
3880 IF Q3$<>"Y" THEN OPEN "ERRORS.LST" FOR APPEND LOCK READ WRITE AS 3:WRITE#3,"ledger MISSING INVOICES IN CHECK.INV  check#",C1$,DATE$:CLOSE 3:INPUT "I can't find the fucking INVOICES!";Q$:RETURN
3890 PRINT"Copying Ledger..."
3898 KILL "ledger.cur":NAME "LEDGER.TMP" AS "LEDGER.CUR"
3899 KILL "check.inv":NAME "CHECK.TMP" AS "CHECK.INV"
3900 CLS:PRINT"All Done.":BEEP:RETURN
3910 OPEN "INVOICE.CHK" SHARED AS #3 LEN=26
3920 FIELD #3,9 AS INUM2$,8 AS AMT2$,8 AS CO$,1 AS FLAG$
3930 FOR X=1 TO CT:HOLD=INT(HOLD(X)-75000!):LOCK#3,HOLD:GET#3,HOLD:LSET FLAG$="J"
3940 PUT#3,HOLD:UNLOCK#3,HOLD:NEXT:PRINT CNUM$;" found & reseting invoices to Un-Paid..."
3950 CLOSE 3
```

That matches other legacy code where `"J"` is used as the normal journal/open state and `"C"` is credit/zero amount. For example, invoice update scripts set `Q$="J"` by default and only switch to `"C"` when amount is zero. îˆ€citeîˆ‚fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82cîˆ

So the correct delete behavior is:

- find the matching `CHECK.INV` block
- for each invoice in that block
- open `INVOICE.CHK`
- seek to the record
- set `FLAG` to `"J"`
- leave the other fields unchanged

I also found several relevant legacy files in `testsubject117/AMiDataStore`, including `CHECK.BAS`, `INVOICE.BAS`, `INVCHK.BAS`, `INVFIX.BAS`, `LEGENTER.ASC`, and `LL.L`. The code-search results are limited and may be incomplete, but `LL.L` is enough to answer this specific question. For broader browsing, you can inspect the repo in GitHubâ€™s code search UI for `INVOICE.CHK` and `FLAG$`, and the repo contents UI for the root listing. 

## What to change now
You need two pieces:

### 1. Add an `INVOICE.CHK` writer/updater
Create a new file like this:

```vbnet
Option Strict On
Option Explicit On

Imports System.IO
Imports System.Text

Public NotInheritable Class InvoiceChkWriter
    Private Sub New()
    End Sub

    Private Const RecordSize As Integer = 26
    Private Const InvoiceBase As Long = 75000L

    Public Shared Sub SetFlag(path As String, invoiceNumber As Long, flag As String)
        If String.IsNullOrEmpty(path) Then Return
        If Not File.Exists(path) Then Return

        Dim filNum As Long = invoiceNumber - InvoiceBase
        If filNum < 1 Then Return

        Dim recordOffset As Long = (filNum - 1) * RecordSize

        Using fs As New FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.ReadWrite)
            If recordOffset + RecordSize > fs.Length Then
                Return
            End If

            fs.Seek(recordOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin)

            Dim buf(RecordSize - 1) As Byte
            Dim bytesRead As Integer = fs.Read(buf, 0, RecordSize)
            If bytesRead < RecordSize Then
                Return
            End If

            Dim flagByte As Byte = 0
            If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(flag) Then
                flagByte = CByte(AscW(flag.Substring(0, 1)))
            End If

            buf(25) = flagByte

            fs.Seek(recordOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin)
            fs.Write(buf, 0, buf.Length)
            fs.Flush()
        End Using
    End Sub
End Class
```

### 2. Update delete flow to reset linked invoices first
In `FormDeleteCheck.vb`, change `DeleteCheck(info)` to:

```vbnet
Private Sub DeleteCheck(info As LedgerEntry)
    ResetInvoicesToUnpaid(info)
    DeleteFromLedger(info)
    DeleteFromCheckInv(info)
    Thread.Sleep(750)
End Sub
```

Then add this method:

```vbnet
Private Sub ResetInvoicesToUnpaid(info As LedgerEntry)
    Dim blocks As List(Of CheckInvBlock) = CheckInvReader.ReadAll(LegacyDataPaths.CheckInv)
    Dim i As Integer

    For i = 0 To blocks.Count - 1
        If CheckInvMatchesLedgerEntry(blocks(i), info) Then
            Dim j As Integer

            For j = 0 To blocks(i).Invoices.Count - 1
                Dim invoiceText As String = blocks(i).Invoices(j)
                Dim invoiceNumber As Long

                If Long.TryParse(invoiceText, invoiceNumber) Then
                    InvoiceChkWriter.SetFlag(LegacyDataPaths.InvoiceChk, invoiceNumber, "J")
                End If
            Next

            Exit For
        End If
    Next
End Sub
```

This assumes you already have `LegacyDataPaths.InvoiceChk`. If not, add it pointing to `INVOICE.CHK`.

## Why this is the right implementation
Because the original delete routine does **not** zero amounts or blank company codes during delete; it only changes the invoice status flag back to `"J"` in `INVOICE.CHK`. îˆ€citeîˆ‚fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82cîˆ

## Likely matching issue
Your legacy code matched only on:
- customer
- check number

Specifically:

```basic
IF CNUM$=C1$ AND N2$=N0$ THEN ...
```

It did **not** require salesman/reference/date/amount to match. îˆ€citeîˆ‚fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82cîˆ

But your current VB helper probably does this stricter match:

- customer
- date
- check number
- salesman/reference
- amount

That is too strict for the reset lookup.

So even if the delete removes the block later, the reset step may fail to find it first.

## Fix
Use a looser match for the invoice-reset step: just customer + check number.

Add this helper to `FormDeleteCheck.vb`:

```vbnet
Private Function CheckInvMatchesForInvoiceReset(block As CheckInvBlock, info As LedgerEntry) As Boolean
    If block Is Nothing OrElse info Is Nothing Then
        Return False
    End If

    If Not String.Equals(block.CustomerCode, info.Customer, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) Then
        Return False
    End If

    If Not String.Equals(block.CheckNumber, info.CheckNumber, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) Then
        Return False
    End If

    Return True
End Function
```

Then change `ResetInvoicesToUnpaid` to use that function:

```vbnet
Private Sub ResetInvoicesToUnpaid(info As LedgerEntry)
    Dim blocks As List(Of CheckInvBlock) = CheckInvReader.ReadAll(LegacyDataPaths.CheckInv)
    Dim i As Integer

    For i = 0 To blocks.Count - 1
        If CheckInvMatchesForInvoiceReset(blocks(i), info) Then
            Dim j As Integer

            For j = 0 To blocks(i).Invoices.Count - 1
                Dim invoiceText As String = blocks(i).Invoices(j)
                Dim invoiceNumber As Long

                If Long.TryParse(invoiceText, invoiceNumber) Then
                    InvoiceChkWriter.SetFlag(LegacyDataPaths.InvoiceChk, invoiceNumber, "J")
                End If
            Next

            Exit For
        End If
    Next
End Sub
```

## Why this is the right fix
Because it matches the legacy delete logic instead of your stricter modern comparison. The old system found the `CHECK.INV` block using only customer and check number before resetting invoice flags. îˆ€citeîˆ‚fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82cîˆ

```
Option Strict On
Option Explicit On

Imports System.IO
Imports System.Linq

Public Class FormFindByInvoiceNumber

    Private Const ScreenTitle As String = "Find by Invoice #"

    Private ReadOnly txtInvoice As New TextBox()
    Private ReadOnly btnFind As New Button()
    Private ReadOnly btnRefresh As New Button()
    Private ReadOnly lblResult As New Label()

    Private _all As List(Of CheckInvBlock) = New List(Of CheckInvBlock)()

    Protected Overrides Sub OnLoad(e As EventArgs)
        MyBase.OnLoad(e)

        Text = "Find by Invoice # (CHECK.INV)"
        Width = 750
        Height = 320
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent

        Dim top As New FlowLayoutPanel() With {.Dock = DockStyle.Top, .Height = 45, .Padding = New Padding(10)}
        top.Controls.Add(New Label() With {.Text = "Invoice #:", .AutoSize = True, .Padding = New Padding(0, 10, 0, 0)})

        txtInvoice.Width = 170
        top.Controls.Add(txtInvoice)

        btnFind.Text = "Find"
        top.Controls.Add(btnFind)

        btnRefresh.Text = "Refresh"
        top.Controls.Add(btnRefresh)

        lblResult.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
        lblResult.Padding = New Padding(10)
        lblResult.Text = "Loading CHECK.INV..."

        Controls.Add(lblResult)
        Controls.Add(top)

        AddHandler btnFind.Click, Sub() DoFind()

        AddHandler btnRefresh.Click,
            Sub()
                LoadData()
            End Sub

        AddHandler txtInvoice.KeyDown,
            Sub(sender, args)
                If args.KeyCode = Keys.Enter Then
                    args.SuppressKeyPress = True
                    DoFind()
                End If
            End Sub

        LoadData()
    End Sub

    Private Sub LoadData()
        Dim path = LegacyDataPaths.CheckInv

        Try
            If Not File.Exists(path) Then
                _all = New List(Of CheckInvBlock)()
                lblResult.Text =
                    "CHECK.INV was not found at:" & Environment.NewLine &
                    path

                UiFileErrors.ShowMissingRequiredFile(Me, ScreenTitle, path)
                Return
            End If

            _all = CheckInvReader.ReadAll(path)
            lblResult.Text = $"Loaded {_all.Count:N0} check records from CHECK.INV."
        Catch ex As Exception
            _all = New List(Of CheckInvBlock)()
            lblResult.Text =
                "Unable to read CHECK.INV from:" & Environment.NewLine &
                path

            UiFileErrors.ShowUnableToReadRequiredFile(Me, ScreenTitle, path, ex)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Sub DoFind()
        Dim invNo = txtInvoice.Text.Trim()
        If invNo = "" Then Return

        If _all Is Nothing OrElse _all.Count = 0 Then
            LoadData()
            If _all Is Nothing OrElse _all.Count = 0 Then Return
        End If

        Dim hit = _all.FirstOrDefault(
            Function(b) b.Invoices IsNot Nothing AndAlso
                        b.Invoices.Any(Function(x) String.Equals(x.Trim(), invNo, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        )

        If hit Is Nothing Then
            lblResult.Text = $"No match for invoice #{invNo}."
            Return
        End If

        lblResult.Text =
            $"Invoice {invNo} was paid by:" & Environment.NewLine &
            $"Customer: {hit.CustomerCode}" & Environment.NewLine &
            $"Check #:  {hit.CheckNumber}" & Environment.NewLine &
            $"Date:     {hit.DateText}" & Environment.NewLine &
            $"Amount:   {hit.Amount:C}" & Environment.NewLine &
            $"Invoices on that check: {hit.InvoiceCount}"
    End Sub

End Class
```

## @testsubject117

```
Option Strict On
Option Explicit On

Imports System.Collections.Generic
Imports System.Globalization
Imports System.IO
Imports System.Text

Public NotInheritable Class CheckInvWriter
    Private Sub New()
    End Sub

    Public Shared Sub WriteAll(path As String, records As IEnumerable(Of CheckInvBlock))
        Dim lines As New List(Of String)()

        For Each record As CheckInvBlock In records
            lines.Add(BuildHeaderLine(record))

            If record.Invoices IsNot Nothing Then
                For Each invoice As String In record.Invoices
                    lines.Add(If(invoice, "").Trim())
                Next
            End If
        Next

        File.WriteAllLines(path, lines, Encoding.ASCII)
    End Sub

    Public Shared Sub Append(path As String, record As CheckInvBlock)
        Using sw As New StreamWriter(path, append:=True, encoding:=Encoding.ASCII)
            sw.WriteLine(BuildHeaderLine(record))

            If record.Invoices IsNot Nothing Then
                For Each invoice As String In record.Invoices
                    sw.WriteLine(If(invoice, "").Trim())
                Next
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function BuildHeaderLine(record As CheckInvBlock) As String
        Dim invoiceCount As Integer = 0
        If record.Invoices IsNot Nothing Then
            invoiceCount = record.Invoices.Count
        End If

        Return String.Join(",",
            Quote(record.CustomerCode),
            Quote(record.CheckNumber),
            Quote(record.SalesmanCode),
            Quote(record.DateText),
            record.Amount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            invoiceCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
    End Function

    Private Shared Function Quote(value As String) As String
        If value Is Nothing Then value = ""
        value = value.Replace("""", """""")
        Return """" & value & """"
    End Function
End Class

```

## Copilot

Found it.

## Direct answer
`CheckInvWriter.vb` is almost certainly the source of the corruption.

### The bug
Your writer is writing invoice numbers as **plain trimmed text lines**:

```vbnet
For Each invoice As String In record.Invoices
    lines.Add(If(invoice, "").Trim())
Next
```

But the legacy `CHECK.INV` file appears to be treated as **GW-BASIC `WRITE#` output**. That means invoice lines may need to be written in the same `WRITE#`-style format the old code used, not just raw lines.

More importantly, your current reader is permissive enough that malformed or unexpected invoice lines can bleed across block boundaries. When combined with plain-text append/write, thatâ€™s how you end up with a tiny fake check owning invoice `724700` and claiming `1519` invoices.

---

# Whatâ€™s wrong structurally
The old BASIC code wrote records like:

```basic
WRITE#1,N2$;CNUM$;RF$;DT$;CHKAMT;CT
FOR X=1 TO CT:WRITE#1,INT(HOLD(X)):NEXT
```

That means **every invoice number line was also written with `WRITE#` semantics**, not as arbitrary trimmed text. The legacy reader expects a â€œCSV-ishâ€ BASIC format. Your current writer only fully respects that for the header, not for the invoice lines. îˆ€citeîˆ‚fc7a4fc2af045364b6d717e3031e74cd9266f82cîˆ

---

## Best fix
Write each invoice line in explicit BASIC-style format too.

For numeric invoice lines, the safest equivalent is:

```text
724700
```

But to stay consistent with `WRITE#`, and avoid weird whitespace/format issues, we should normalize aggressively and only emit valid invoice numbers, one per line.

Also, we should ensure:
- no blank invoice lines get written
- header count equals actual valid invoice count written

Right now, if `record.Invoices` contains blanks, garbage, or inherited data, the count and content can drift.

---

# Replace `CheckInvWriter.vb` with this
This version:
- filters invoice lines to valid numeric invoice tokens only
- writes a correct count based on filtered invoices
- writes clean one-per-line invoice numbers
- avoids blank garbage lines

```vbnet
Option Strict On
Option Explicit On

Imports System.Collections.Generic
Imports System.Globalization
Imports System.IO
Imports System.Text

Public NotInheritable Class CheckInvWriter
    Private Sub New()
    End Sub

    Public Shared Sub WriteAll(path As String, records As IEnumerable(Of CheckInvBlock))
        Dim lines As New List(Of String)()

        For Each record As CheckInvBlock In records
            Dim invoices As List(Of String) = NormalizeInvoices(record)

            lines.Add(BuildHeaderLine(record, invoices.Count))

            Dim i As Integer
            For i = 0 To invoices.Count - 1
                lines.Add(invoices(i))
            Next
        Next

        File.WriteAllLines(path, lines, Encoding.ASCII)
    End Sub

    Public Shared Sub Append(path As String, record As CheckInvBlock)
        Dim invoices As List(Of String) = NormalizeInvoices(record)

        Using sw As New StreamWriter(path, append:=True, encoding:=Encoding.ASCII)
            sw.WriteLine(BuildHeaderLine(record, invoices.Count))

            Dim i As Integer
            For i = 0 To invoices.Count - 1
                sw.WriteLine(invoices(i))
            Next
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Shared Function NormalizeInvoices(record As CheckInvBlock) As List(Of String)
        Dim result As New List(Of String)()

        If record Is Nothing OrElse record.Invoices Is Nothing Then
            Return result
        End If

        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To record.Invoices.Count - 1
            Dim raw As String = If(record.Invoices(i), "").Replace(ChrW(26), "").Trim()

            If raw = "" Then
                Continue For
            End If

            Dim n As Long
            If Long.TryParse(raw, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, n) OrElse
               Long.TryParse(raw, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, n) Then
                result.Add(n.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
            End If
        Next

        Return result
    End Function

    Private Shared Function BuildHeaderLine(record As CheckInvBlock, invoiceCount As Integer) As String
        Return String.Join(",",
            Quote(If(record.CustomerCode, "")),
            Quote(If(record.CheckNumber, "")),
            Quote(If(record.SalesmanCode, "")),
            Quote(If(record.DateText, "")),
            record.Amount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            invoiceCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
    End Function

    Private Shared Function Quote(value As String) As String
        If value Is Nothing Then value = ""
        value = value.Replace("""", """""")
        Return """" & value & """"
    End Function
End Class
```

---

# Why this matters
Your fake test check likely got written with a huge or dirty inherited `Invoices` list, or with invoice data that wasnâ€™t normalized before write. Then the reader accepted that block as legitimate and the invoice-find screen exposed it.

This new version prevents that by:
- only writing numeric invoice lines
- recomputing the count from what is actually written
